<script>
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
        const searchFieldEl = document.getElementById("search-field");

        if (!searchFieldEl) return; 
        const regexp =  /^[\p{L}\u0900-\u097F\s]+$/u;

        searchFieldEl.addEventListener("paste", (e) => {
            const value = e.target.value;
            const clipboardData = e.clipboardData || window.clipboardData;
            const clipboardVal = clipboardData.getData("text/plain");
            
            const isValid = regexp.test(clipboardVal);
            if (!isValid)  e.preventDefault();
        });

        searchFieldEl.addEventListener("keydown", (e) => {
            const key = e.key;

            const isValid = regexp.test(key);
            if (!isValid)  e.preventDefault();
        })
    });
</script>

            <summary class="oap-collapsible__title"
                     v-tag:useractionevent='{
                      category: "productpagetab",
                      event_name : "body_button_click",
                      productTab :"HOW TO USE",
                      productInfo: "true-match-nude-hyaluronic-tinted-serum-hydrates-all-in-1::071249635919",
                      action: "HOW TO USE",
                      label: "true-match-nude-hyaluronic-tinted-serum-hydrates-all-in-1",

                HOW TO USE
            </summary>
            <div class="oap-collapsible__content">

<div class="rich-text">
    <div class="component-content">
        <div class="field-text"><p>
Achieve a radiant, natural finish with Hyaluronic Tinted Serum in a few simple steps:
</p>
<p>
<strong>Step 1: Shake Well.</strong><br />
Before application, shake the bottle thoroughly to ensure the formula is evenly mixed and ready for use.
</p>
<p>
<strong>Step 2: Use the Dropper.</strong><br />
Use the dropper to pick up serum and brush off any excess inside the bottle. Add a few drops of the serum to your preferred method of application&mdash;this can be your hands, a foundation brush, or a makeup blender.
</p>
<p>
<strong>Step 3: Blend with Your Preferred Tool.</strong><br />
Use one of the following methods to blend for a seamless finish:
</p>
<ul>
    <li>
    <strong>With Your Hands:</strong> Rub the serum into your skin as you would any face serum. Gently smooth it across your face, starting from the center and moving outward.
    </li>
    <li>
    <strong>With a Makeup Blender:</strong> Using gentle bouncing and buffing motions, apply the serum to your skin with a damp makeup blender, ensuring an even, natural finish.
    </li>
    <li>
    <strong>With a Brush:</strong> Use a foundation or stippling brush to blend outward in gentle, circular motions for a smooth, streak-free application.
    </li>
</ul>
<p style="margin-top: 1rem;">
<strong>Step 4: Build and Seal.</strong><br />
Add additional layers to achieve your desired level of coverage, from sheer to medium. Use gentle tapping motions to blend seamlessly for a radiant, hydrated finish.
</p>
<p>

</p></div>    </div>

	<strong>Hyaluronic Acid</strong><br />
	<strong>What it does:</strong> Known for its intensely hydrating and replumping properties, HA forms an imperceptible film on the skin which prevents moisture loss.<br />
	<strong>Why it matters:</strong> This potent hydration-attracting molecule is naturally found in the skin, but we lose it as we age, which leads to lack of firmness and dryness.

<bv-scroll-tagging :tagging-data="{&quot;event&quot;:&quot;uaevent&quot;,&quot;eventCategory&quot;:&quot;product detail page&quot;,&quot;eventAction&quot;:&quot;read review&quot;,&quot;ecommerce&quot;:&quot;undefined&quot;,&quot;event_name&quot;:&quot;read_review&quot;,&quot;eventLabel&quot;:&quot;nude hyaluronic tinted serum foundation with 1% hyaluronic acid, lightweight coverage for dry skin::071249635919&quot;,&quot;product_info&quot;:&quot;nude hyaluronic tinted serum foundation with 1% hyaluronic acid, lightweight coverage for dry skin::071249635919&quot;}"></bv-scroll-tagging>


<script>
        var bvEventsConfigured = false;

        function bvSubmissionLoad(data) {
            const { contentType } = data;
            let writeReviewTag = {
                "event": "uaevent",
                "event_name": "write_review",
                "ecommerce": "undefined",
                "eventAction": "write review",
                "eventCategory": "product detail page",
                "eventLabel": "nude hyaluronic tinted serum foundation with 1% hyaluronic acid, lightweight coverage for dry skin::071249635919",
                "product_info": "nude hyaluronic tinted serum foundation with 1% hyaluronic acid, lightweight coverage for dry skin::071249635919"
            };
            switch (contentType) {
                case "review":
                    setTimeout(() => {
                        window.dataLayer.push(writeReviewTag);
                    }, 500);
                    break;
                default:
                    break;
            }
        }

        function reviewSubmitted(data) {
            if (data.contentType === "review") {
                setTimeout(() => {
                    window.dataLayer.push({
                        event: "uaevent",
                        event_name: "submit_review",
                        ecommerce: "undefined",
                        eventAction: "submit a review",
                        eventCategory: "product detail page",
                        eventLabel: "nude hyaluronic tinted serum foundation with 1% hyaluronic acid, lightweight coverage for dry skin::071249635919",
                        product_info: "nude hyaluronic tinted serum foundation with 1% hyaluronic acid, lightweight coverage for dry skin::071249635919"
                    });
                }, 500);
            }
        }

        function configureBvEvents(BV) {
            if (bvEventsConfigured || !BV || !BV.configure) {
                return;
            }
            bvEventsConfigured = true;
            BV.configure('global', {
                events: {
                    submissionLoad: function (data) {
                        bvSubmissionLoad(data);
                    },
                    submissionSubmitted: function (data) {
                        reviewSubmitted(data);
                    }
                }
            });
        }

        window.bvCallback = configureBvEvents;

        (function registerBvEventsOnLazyLoad() {
            var scriptSrc = 'https://apps.bazaarvoice.com/deployments/loreal/main_site/production/en_US/bv.js';
            var configureWhenReady = function () {
                configureBvEvents(window.BV || window.$BV);
            };

            if (window.$INJECTED_URLS && window.$INJECTED_URLS[scriptSrc]) {
                window.$INJECTED_URLS[scriptSrc].then(configureWhenReady);
                return;
            }

            var attempts = 0;
            var interval = setInterval(function () {
                attempts++;
                if (window.$INJECTED_URLS && window.$INJECTED_URLS[scriptSrc]) {
                    clearInterval(interval);
                    window.$INJECTED_URLS[scriptSrc].then(configureWhenReady);
                } else if (window.BV || window.$BV) {
                    clearInterval(interval);
                    configureWhenReady();
                } else if (attempts >= 60) {
                    clearInterval(interval);
                }
            }, 500);
        })();
</script>
